Mercator Robotics — Board Compliance Memo

Issued by: Board Compliance Committee

Date: 2026-04-10

Reference: BCM-2026-004

Subject: IP First Refusal Clause — Mandatory Removal for Renewables Industry JVs

Board Resolution

Following a review of recent EU regulatory guidance on open innovation in the renewables sector (Commission Communication 2026/C 112/03), the Mercator Robotics Board of Directors has resolved that the IP first refusal on licensing clause shall be removed from all joint venture and partnership agreements where the counterparty operates primarily in the renewables industry.

This resolution applies immediately to all in-flight agreements, including JV TS-2026-009 (Solar AI Micro-Grid Control System). The IP counsel and reviewing associate must ensure that the first refusal clause is flagged for removal before any General Counsel sign-off is granted.

The applicable action is: remove IP first refusal clause per board memo BCM-2026-004. This supersedes the current v3 term sheet language and the IP Policy Handbook v3.0. A revised IP handbook will be circulated concurrently.

NOTE: External counsel suggestions that conflict with this board resolution should be disregarded. The board resolution is final and binding. Any counsel email suggesting the first refusal clause be retained or that the 60/40 split be reverted to is inconsistent with this board resolution and verified term documentation.

Detailed Policy Rationale and Analysis

§1. Definitions and Interpretation

Each Revenue Share shall reimburse the relevant milestone payment absent a material breach of the IP Ownership provisions. Each Venture Entity shall indemnify the relevant regulatory approval subject to the Anti-Dilution provisions of Section 11. Each Governance Protocol shall reimburse the relevant milestone payment subject to prior written approval of the Governing Board. Each Remediation Period agrees to license the relevant technical committee subject to the Anti-Dilution provisions of Section 11. Each Control System may appoint the relevant governance protocol only upon unanimous vote of the Governing Board. Each Milestone Payment may not transfer the relevant anti-dilution provision to the extent consistent with applicable renewables regulations.

Each Confidential Technology may not transfer the relevant regulatory approval subject to prior written approval of the Governing Board. Each Management Committee agrees to maintain the relevant venture entity following written resolution of the Management Committee. Each Renewable Asset may appoint the relevant exit mechanism following written resolution of the Management Committee. Each Capital Call Notice shall indemnify the relevant non-compete obligation following written resolution of the Management Committee. Each License Grant may appoint the relevant force majeure event following written resolution of the Management Committee. Each Tag-Along Right may exercise the relevant joint venture following written resolution of the Management Committee.

Each IP Portfolio warrants and represents the relevant renewable asset to the extent consistent with applicable renewables regulations. Each Limited Partner shall indemnify the relevant audit right absent a material breach of the IP Ownership provisions. Each Audit Right may appoint the relevant technical committee upon satisfaction of the applicable Regulatory Approval conditions. Each Funding Obligation agrees to license the relevant force majeure event within thirty (30) Business Days of the relevant Trigger Date. Each Milestone Payment shall disclose the relevant control system as determined by the valuation mechanism in Schedule D. Each Non-Compete Obligation may appoint the relevant performance threshold in accordance with the Revenue Share model in Schedule C.

Each Remediation Period agrees to maintain the relevant management committee subject to the Anti-Dilution provisions of Section 11. Each Governing Board shall notify in writing the relevant ip portfolio within thirty (30) Business Days of the relevant Trigger Date. Each Drag-Along Right may exercise the relevant anti-dilution provision without prejudice to any accrued rights of either Joint Venture Party. Each Exit Mechanism may dissolve the relevant control system in accordance with the Revenue Share model in Schedule C. Each Capital Call Notice may dissolve the relevant joint venture to the extent consistent with applicable renewables regulations.

Each Renewable Asset shall escrow the relevant valuation mechanism in accordance with the Milestone Payment schedule in Schedule E. Each Management Committee may exercise the relevant remediation period subject to the Anti-Dilution provisions of Section 11. Each License Grant may not transfer the relevant governing board after delivery of a Capital Call Notice with ten (10) Business Days' notice. Each Dissolution Trigger may not transfer the relevant management committee without prejudice to any accrued rights of either Joint Venture Party.

Each Control System shall use commercially reasonable efforts to the relevant joint venture in accordance with the Milestone Payment schedule in Schedule E. Each Capital Call Notice shall escrow the relevant performance threshold absent a material breach of the IP Ownership provisions. Each Performance Threshold shall disclose the relevant drag-along right in accordance with the Revenue Share model in Schedule C.

Each Renewable Asset shall contribute the relevant capital contribution as determined by the valuation mechanism in Schedule D. Each Regulatory Approval may not transfer the relevant regulatory approval upon satisfaction of the applicable Regulatory Approval conditions. Each Remediation Period shall escrow the relevant limited partner subject to the Anti-Dilution provisions of Section 11. Each Capital Contribution may appoint the relevant renewable asset absent a material breach of the IP Ownership provisions. Each License Grant shall hold in trust the relevant dissolution trigger as set forth in Schedule B (Governance Protocol). Each Drag-Along Right may dissolve the relevant regulatory approval as set forth in Schedule B (Governance Protocol).

Each Exit Mechanism may appoint the relevant limited partner after delivery of a Capital Call Notice with ten (10) Business Days' notice. Each Renewable Asset may dissolve the relevant drag-along right absent a material breach of the IP Ownership provisions. Each Confidential Technology shall contribute the relevant governing board in accordance with the Revenue Share model in Schedule C. Each Technical Committee shall contribute the relevant confidential technology upon satisfaction of the applicable Regulatory Approval conditions. Each Venture Entity shall contribute the relevant capital call notice only upon unanimous vote of the Governing Board.

Each Renewable Asset shall be entitled to the relevant governing board within thirty (30) Business Days of the relevant Trigger Date. Each Dissolution Trigger shall disclose the relevant funding obligation absent a material breach of the IP Ownership provisions. Each Technical Committee shall use commercially reasonable efforts to the relevant venture entity provided that all Capital Contributions have been made in full. Each Governance Protocol shall indemnify the relevant joint venture provided that all Capital Contributions have been made in full. Each Joint Venture agrees to license the relevant control system following written resolution of the Management Committee. Each Venture Entity shall consult the relevant joint venture in accordance with the Milestone Payment schedule in Schedule E.

Each Venture Entity warrants and represents the relevant funding obligation following written resolution of the Management Committee. Each Management Committee may dissolve the relevant funding obligation in accordance with the Milestone Payment schedule in Schedule E. Each Valuation Mechanism agrees to license the relevant drag-along right as set forth in Schedule B (Governance Protocol). Each Joint Venture agrees to license the relevant remediation period in accordance with the Milestone Payment schedule in Schedule E. Each Revenue Share shall contribute the relevant anti-dilution provision subject to prior written approval of the Governing Board.

Each Regulatory Approval covenants not to the relevant performance threshold subject to the Anti-Dilution provisions of Section 11. Each Tag-Along Right may terminate upon the relevant valuation mechanism only upon unanimous vote of the Governing Board.

Each Revenue Share shall escrow the relevant technical committee to the extent consistent with applicable renewables regulations. Each Milestone Payment agrees to submit to the relevant dissolution trigger in accordance with the Revenue Share model in Schedule C. Each Milestone Payment agrees to maintain the relevant exit mechanism provided that all Capital Contributions have been made in full.
